local _aimStartMousePos = nil

local _smoothDampAngle = (function(current, target, vel, smoothTime, dt)
    smoothTime = math.max(0.0001, smoothTime)
    local omega = 2 / smoothTime
    local change = current - target
    local expf = math.exp(-omega * dt)
    local temp = (vel + omega * change) * dt
    vel = (vel - omega * temp) * expf
    return target + (change + temp) * expf, vel
end)

local _easeCache = { style = "Quad", dir = "Out", sharp = 0.2, back = 1.7, bounce = 1, elasticPeriod = 0.3, elasticity = 1.3, swaySpeed = 1.5, swayWidth = 0.1, jitterAmt = 0.075 }
local _easeCacheFrame = -1

local applyEasing = function(t)
    t = math.clamp(t, 0, 1)
    local f = _easeCacheFrame
    if f ~= _aimFrameSkip then
        _easeCacheFrame = _aimFrameSkip
        local char = lp.Character
        local hum = char and char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
        local inAir = hum and hum.FloorMaterial == Enum.Material.Air
        _easeCache.style = Flags["EasingType"] or "Quad"
        _easeCache.dir = Flags["EasingDirection"] or "Out"
        _easeCache.amount = math.clamp(Flags["EasingAmount"] or 50, 1, 100) / 100
        _easeCache.back = math.clamp((Flags["EasingBack"] or 17) / 10, 0, 5)
        _easeCache.bounce = math.clamp(Flags["EasingBounce"] or 1, 0.1, 20)
        _easeCache.elasticPeriod = math.clamp((Flags["EasingElasticPeriod"] or 30) / 100, 0.05, 2)
        _easeCache.elasticity = math.clamp((Flags["EasingElasticity"] or 130) / 100, 0, 5)
        _easeCache.sharp = math.clamp((Flags["EasingSharp"] or 20) / 100, 0.01, 1)
    end
    local style = _easeCache.style
    local dir = _easeCache.dir
    local amount = _easeCache.amount
    local back = _easeCache.back
    local bounce = _easeCache.bounce
    local elasticPeriod = _easeCache.elasticPeriod
    local elasticity = _easeCache.elasticity
    local sharp = _easeCache.sharp

    local eased
    if style == "Linear" then
        eased = t
    elseif style == "Back" then
        -- Back easing with proper direction support
        local backAmt = back * 1.70158
        if dir == "In" then
            eased = t * t * ((backAmt + 1) * t - backAmt)
        elseif dir == "InOut" then
            local s = backAmt * 1.525
            if t < 0.5 then
                eased = 0.5 * ((2 * t) * (2 * t) * ((s + 1) * 2 * t - s))
            else
                eased = 0.5 * ((2 * t - 2) * (2 * t - 2) * ((s + 1) * (t * 2 - 2) + s) + 2)
            end
        else -- Out (default)
            -- Standard back-out: overshoots at start, settles at end
            eased = 1 + (1 - t) * (1 - t) * ((backAmt + 1) * (1 - t) - backAmt)
        end
    elseif style == "Bounce" then
        -- Bounce easing
        if dir == "In" then
            eased = 1 - (1 - t) * (1 - t) * (1 - t) * bounce * 0.3
            if eased < 0 then eased = 0 end
        elseif dir == "InOut" then
            if t < 0.5 then
                eased = 0.5 - 0.5 * (1 - 2 * t) * (1 - 2 * t) * (1 - 2 * t) * bounce * 0.3
            else
                eased = 0.5 + 0.5 * (2 * t - 1) * (2 * t - 1) * (2 * t - 1) * bounce * 0.3
            end
        else -- Out
            local inv = 1 - t
            eased = 1 - (inv * inv * inv * inv) * bounce * 0.2
            if eased < 0 then eased = 0 end
        end
    elseif style == "Elastic" then
        if t == 0 or t == 1 then
            eased = t
        else
            local p = elasticPeriod
            local s = p / 4
            if dir == "In" then
                eased = -math.pow(2, 10 * (t - 1)) * math.sin((t - 1 - s) * (2 * math.pi) / p) * elasticity
            elseif dir == "InOut" then
                if t < 0.5 then
                    eased = -0.5 * math.pow(2, 20 * t - 10) * math.sin((20 * t - 11.125) * (2 * math.pi) / p) * elasticity
                else
                    eased = 0.5 * math.pow(2, -20 * t + 10) * math.sin((20 * t - 11.125) * (2 * math.pi) / p) * elasticity + 1
                end
            else -- Out (default)
                eased = math.pow(2, -10 * t) * math.sin((t - s) * (2 * math.pi) / p) * elasticity + 1
            end
        end
    elseif style == "Trim" then
        eased = math.min(1, t * (1 + sharp))
    elseif style == "Trace" then
        eased = math.min(1, t ^ (0.8 - sharp * 0.1))
    elseif style == "Focus" then
        local inv = 1 - t
        eased = 1 - (inv * inv * inv)
    elseif style == "Flick" then
        local inv = 1 - t
        eased = 1 - (inv * inv * inv * inv * inv)
    else
        local success, val = pcall(function()
            local es = Enum.EasingStyle[style]
            local ed = Enum.EasingDirection[dir]
            return TweenService:GetValue(t, es, ed)
        end)
        if success then
            eased = val
        else
            local inv = 1 - t
            eased = 1 - (inv * inv * inv * inv)
        end
    end
    return t + (eased - t) * amount
end

_prevAimTarget = nil
_aimbotMissOffset = Vector3.new(0, 0, 0)
_aimbotShouldMiss = false
_reactionTimer = nil
_missChanceTarget = nil

resetPredictionState = function()
    _targetStates = setmetatable({}, { __mode = "k" })
end

task.spawn(function()
    task.wait(2)
    _trackConn(RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(_LPH_NV(function(dt)
        _aimFrameSkip = _aimFrameSkip + 1
        _aimbotSetCamera = false
        local aimbotActive = _bindActive("AimbotBind")
        if not aimbotActive then
            _prevAimTarget = nil
            _aimbotShouldMiss = false
            _aimbotMissOffset = Vector3.new(0, 0, 0)
            _missChanceTarget = nil
            lockedAimTarget = nil
            _delayJumpCount = 0
            _delayJumpTimer = 0
            _fallDelayTimer = 0
            _aimTargetAcquireTime = nil
            _aimWasEnabled = false
            _reactionTimer = nil
            aimbotTarget = nil
            _lastAimbotTargetPos = nil
            _lastAimbotCF = nil
            _lastAimbotTargetPlayer = nil
            _lockedResolvedPart = nil
            _lockedResolvedHitPart = nil
            _hasLockedTargetDuringThisPress = false
            _targetDiedDuringThisPress = false
            _smoothedAimPos = nil
            _aimStartCF = nil
            _mouseRemainderX = 0
            _mouseRemainderY = 0
            _aimVelPitch = 0
            _aimVelYaw = 0
            _smoothedPredictedPos = nil
            _currentAirOffset = 0
            _lastTargetInAir = false
            _aimEaseProgress = 0
            _aimStartMousePos = nil
            -- Reset smooth pull resistance state
            _lastUserMousePos = nil
            _userMouseDeltaX = 0
            _userMouseDeltaY = 0
            _smoothPullAccumX = 0
            _smoothPullAccumY = 0
            _smoothPullUserInfluenceX = 0
            _smoothPullUserInfluenceY = 0
            resetPredictionState()
            restorePlayerCameraSubject()
            return
        end
        if _macroActive then return end
        if not lp.Character or not lp.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") then return end
        local justActivated = not _aimWasEnabled
        _aimWasEnabled = true
        if justActivated then
            _aimEaseProgress = 0
            _aimStartCF = camera.CFrame
            _aimStartMousePos = getMousePos()
            _reactionTimer = tick()
            _lastReactionTimerCheck = tick() -- Track when we set it
        end
        local target = nil
        local fromLockedTarget = false
        local aimChecks = Flags["AimChecks"] or {}
        local checkDead = hasCheck(aimChecks, "Dead")
        local deadCheckTarget = lockedAimTarget or aimbotTarget
        local targetDied = false

        if deadCheckTarget then
            local char
            if deadCheckTarget:IsA("Player") then
                char = deadCheckTarget.Character
            elseif deadCheckTarget:IsA("BasePart") then
                char = deadCheckTarget.Parent
            else
                char = deadCheckTarget.Parent
            end
            if char then
                local shouldUnlock = not char:IsDescendantOf(workspace)
                if not shouldUnlock and Flags["AimStopDead"] then
                    local hum = char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
                    if (hum and (hum.Health < 1 or hum:GetState() == Enum.HumanoidStateType.Dead)) or isKnockedOrKO(char) or isDeadCheck(char) then
                        shouldUnlock = true
                        targetDied = true
                    end
                end
                if shouldUnlock then
                    lockedAimTarget = nil
                    aimbotTarget = nil
                    target = nil
                    _prevAimTarget = nil
                    _lastTarget = nil
                    _smoothedAimPos = nil
                    _aimStartCF = nil
                    _aimStartMousePos = nil
                    _aimEaseProgress = 0
                    _unlockFade = 1
                    _mouseRemainderX = 0
                    _mouseRemainderY = 0
                    _aimVelPitch = 0
                    _aimVelYaw = 0
                    _smoothedPredictedPos = nil
                    _currentAirOffset = 0
                    _lastTargetInAir = false
                    _lockedResolvedPart = nil
                    _lockedResolvedHitPart = nil
                    resetPredictionState()
                    if targetDied then
                        _targetDiedDuringThisPress = true
                        restorePlayerCameraSubject()
                    end
                    return
                end
            end
        end

        if (Flags["LockTarget"] or Flags["StickyAim"]) and lockedAimTarget then
            local char = lockedAimTarget.Parent
            if char and char.Parent then
                local hum = char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
                if hum and hum.Health > 0 and hum:GetState() ~= Enum.HumanoidStateType.Dead and not isKnockedOrKO(char) and not isDeadCheck(char) and not hasSpawnProtection(char) then
                    if lockedAimTarget:IsDescendantOf(char) then
                        target = lockedAimTarget
                    else
                        lockedAimTarget = nil
                    end
                else
                    lockedAimTarget = nil
                    _targetDiedDuringThisPress = true
                end
            else
                lockedAimTarget = nil
            end
        end

        if not target then
            if not _targetDiedDuringThisPress then
                _hasLockedTargetDuringThisPress = false
                target = findAimbotTarget()
                if target then
                    _aimTargetAcquireTime = _aimTargetAcquireTime or tick()
                    if Flags["StickyAim"] or Flags["LockTarget"] then
                        lockedAimTarget = target
                        _hasLockedTargetDuringThisPress = true
                    end
                end
            end
        end
        if target then
            if target ~= _prevAimTarget then
                _aimEaseProgress = 0
                _aimStartCF = camera.CFrame
                _aimStartMousePos = getMousePos()
                if Flags["UseUnlockSmoothing"] or Flags["UnlockSmoothing"] then
                    _unlockFade = math.clamp((Flags["UnlockIn"] or 15) / 100, 0.01, 0.99)
                end
            end
            _prevAimTarget = target
            _lastTarget = target
            _lastAimbotTargetLostTime = nil
        else
            local useUnlockDelay = Flags["UnlockDelayEnabled"] or Flags["UnlockDelay"]
            local holdTime = useUnlockDelay and (Flags["UnlockDelayMs"] or 100) / 1000 or 0.15
            if not _lastAimbotTargetLostTime then
                _lastAimbotTargetLostTime = tick()
            end
            local timeSinceLost = tick() - _lastAimbotTargetLostTime
            local keepTracking = false
            if timeSinceLost < holdTime then
                if _lastTarget and _lastTarget.Parent and _lastTarget.Parent.Parent then
                    local char = _lastTarget.Parent
                    local hum = char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
                    local isDead = not hum or (hum.Health < 1 or hum:GetState() == Enum.HumanoidStateType.Dead) or isKnockedOrKO(char) or isDeadCheck(char)
                    if not (isDead and not Flags["StayOnDeathPos"] and not Flags["StayOnDeadspot"]) then
                        target = _lastTarget
                        keepTracking = true
                        if Flags["UseUnlockSmoothing"] or Flags["UnlockSmoothing"] then
                            local unlockOut = math.clamp(Flags["UnlockOut"] or 15, 1, 100)
                            _unlockFade = math.clamp(1 - (timeSinceLost / holdTime) * (unlockOut / 15), 0, 1)
                        end
                    end
                end
            end
            if not keepTracking then
                aimbotTarget = nil
                local targetJustDied = false
                if _lastTarget and _lastTarget.Parent then
                    local char = _lastTarget.Parent
                    local hum = char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
                    local isDead = not hum or (hum.Health < 1 or hum:GetState() == Enum.HumanoidStateType.Dead) or isKnockedOrKO(char) or isDeadCheck(char)
                    if isDead then
                        targetJustDied = true
                    end
                end
                if not (Flags["StayOnDeathPos"] and targetJustDied) then _lastAimbotTargetPos = nil end
                _lastAimbotTargetPlayer = nil
                _lockedResolvedPart = nil
                _lockedResolvedHitPart = nil
                _smoothedAimPos = nil
                _aimStartCF = nil
                _mouseRemainderX = 0
                _mouseRemainderY = 0
                _aimVelPitch = 0
                _aimVelYaw = 0
                _smoothedPredictedPos = nil
                _currentAirOffset = 0
                _lastTargetInAir = false
                _aimEaseProgress = 0
                _aimStartMousePos = nil
                resetPredictionState()
                if Flags["StayOnDeathPos"] and _lastAimbotTargetPos and _targetDiedDuringThisPress then
                    local camCF = camera.CFrame
                    local dir = (_lastAimbotTargetPos - camCF.Position)
                    if dir.Magnitude > 0.1 then
                        local desiredCF = CFrame.new(camCF.Position, _lastAimbotTargetPos)
                        local smoothVal = Flags["DeathPosSmooth"] or 10
                        local rate = math.clamp(1 - (smoothVal / 100), 0.05, 0.99)
                        local damp = 1 - math.pow(1 - rate, dt * 60)
                        camera.CFrame = camCF:Lerp(desiredCF, damp)
                    end
                end
                return
            end
        end
        if not target then
            if Flags["AimStopDead"] and _targetDiedDuringThisPress then
                _lastTarget = nil
                _lastAimbotTargetPos = nil
                _lastAimbotTargetPlayer = nil
                _lockedResolvedPart = nil
                _lockedResolvedHitPart = nil
                _prevAimTarget = nil
                aimbotTarget = nil
                _smoothedAimPos = nil
                _aimStartCF = nil
                _mouseRemainderX = 0
                _mouseRemainderY = 0
                _smoothedPredictedPos = nil
                _aimVelPitch = 0
                _aimVelYaw = 0
                _currentAirOffset = 0
                _lastTargetInAir = false
                _aimEaseProgress = 0
                _unlockFade = 1
                _aimStartMousePos = nil
                resetPredictionState()
                return
            end
            if Flags["StayOnDeathPos"] and _lastAimbotTargetPos and _targetDiedDuringThisPress then
                local camCF = camera.CFrame
                local dir = (_lastAimbotTargetPos - camCF.Position)
                if dir.Magnitude > 0.1 then
                    local desiredCF = CFrame.new(camCF.Position, _lastAimbotTargetPos)
                    local smoothVal = Flags["DeathPosSmooth"] or 10
                    local rate = math.clamp(1 - (smoothVal / 100), 0.05, 0.99)
                    local damp = 1 - math.pow(1 - rate, dt * 60)
                    camera.CFrame = camCF:Lerp(desiredCF, damp)
                end
                return
            end
            _prevAimTarget = nil
            aimbotTarget = nil
            if not (Flags["StayOnDeathPos"] and _targetDiedDuringThisPress) then _lastAimbotTargetPos = nil end
            _lastAimbotTargetPlayer = nil
            _lockedResolvedPart = nil
            _lockedResolvedHitPart = nil
            _smoothedAimPos = nil
            _aimStartCF = nil
            _mouseRemainderX = 0
            _mouseRemainderY = 0
            _smoothedPredictedPos = nil
            _aimVelPitch = 0
            _aimVelYaw = 0
            _currentAirOffset = 0
            _lastTargetInAir = false
            _aimEaseProgress = 0
            _unlockFade = 1
            _aimStartMousePos = nil
            resetPredictionState()
            return
        end
        if target ~= _prevAimTarget then
            _aimEaseProgress = 0
            _unlockFade = 1
            _prevAimTarget = target
            _aimStartCF = camera.CFrame
            _aimStartMousePos = getMousePos()
            _smoothedAimPos = nil
            _currentPartOffset = nil
            _smoothedPredictedPos = nil
            _aimVelPitch = 0
            _aimVelYaw = 0
            _currentAirOffset = 0
            _lastTargetInAir = false
            _lockedResolvedPart = nil
            _lockedResolvedHitPart = nil
            _reactionTimer = tick()
            resetPredictionState()
        elseif Flags["UseEasing"] and _lastAimbotTargetPos then
            local distMoved = (target.Position - _lastAimbotTargetPos).Magnitude
            if distMoved > 50 then
                _aimEaseProgress = 0
                _aimStartCF = camera.CFrame
                _aimStartMousePos = UserInputService:GetMouseLocation()
            end
        end

        if Flags["UseMissChance"] then
            local missPct = math.clamp(Flags["MissChance"] or 0, 0, 100)
            if missPct <= 0 then
                _aimbotShouldMiss = false
                _aimbotMissOffset = Vector3.new(0, 0, 0)
                _missChanceTarget = target
            elseif not _missChanceTarget or _missChanceTarget ~= target then
                _missChanceTarget = target
                local roll = math.random() * 100
                _aimbotShouldMiss = roll <= missPct
                if _aimbotShouldMiss then
                    _aimbotMissOffset = Vector3.new(
                        (math.random() - 0.5) * 6,
                        (math.random() - 0.5) * 6,
                        (math.random() - 0.5) * 6
                    )
                else
                    _aimbotMissOffset = Vector3.new(0, 0, 0)
                end
            end
        else
            _aimbotShouldMiss = false
            _aimbotMissOffset = Vector3.new(0, 0, 0)
            _missChanceTarget = target
        end

        if Flags["UseReactionTime"] and _reactionTimer then
            local elapsed = (tick() - _reactionTimer) * 1000
            local reactionDelay = Flags["ReactionTimeMs"] or 150
            if elapsed < reactionDelay then
                -- During reaction delay, just track target but don't aim
                _lastAimbotTargetPos = aimPos
                return
            end
            _reactionTimer = nil
        end
        if Flags["DelayJump"] or Flags["UseJumpDelay"] then
            local targetChar = target.Parent
            if targetChar then
                local targetHum = targetChar:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
                if targetHum then
                    local targetRoot = targetChar:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                    local vel = targetRoot and targetRoot.AssemblyLinearVelocity or Vector3.new()
                    local humState = targetHum:GetState()
                    local isAirborneNow = targetHum.FloorMaterial == Enum.Material.Air
                        or humState == Enum.HumanoidStateType.Jumping
                        or humState == Enum.HumanoidStateType.Freefall
                        or humState == Enum.HumanoidStateType.FallingDown
                        or math.abs(vel.Y) > 0.15
                    if isAirborneNow and not _lastTargetInAir then
                        _delayJumpTimer = tick()
                    end
                    _lastTargetInAir = isAirborneNow
                    if isAirborneNow and _delayJumpTimer ~= 0 then
                        local elapsed = (tick() - _delayJumpTimer) * 1000
                        if elapsed < (Flags["DelayJumpMs"] or 50) then
                            return
                        end
                    end
                    if not isAirborneNow then
                        _delayJumpTimer = 0
                    end
                end
            end
        end

        local targetParent = target and target.Parent
        local plr = targetParent and Players:GetPlayerFromCharacter(targetParent)
        if plr then aimbotTarget = plr else aimbotTarget = nil end

        local tpTargetActive = Flags["TPTarget"] == true or (_bindActive and _bindActive("TPTargetBind"))
        if tpTargetActive and aimbotTarget and aimbotTarget.Character then
            local localChar = lp.Character
            local targetChar = aimbotTarget.Character
            local localHum = localChar and localChar:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
            local localHrp = localChar and localChar:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
            local targetHrp = targetChar and targetChar:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
            if localHum and localHrp and targetHrp then
                if _tpLastHealth and localHum.Health < _tpLastHealth then
                    _tpHideUntil = tick() + (Flags["TPHideInterval"] or 0)
                end
                _tpLastHealth = localHum.Health
                local armOnly = Flags["TPArmOnly"] == true
                local hideUntil = _tpHideUntil or 0
                local tpType = Flags["TPType"] or "Static"
                local tpAmount = Flags["TPAmount"] or 3
                local orbit = Flags["OrbitTarget"] == true
                local orbitSpeed = Flags["OrbitSpeed"] or 8
                if tick() < hideUntil then
                    if not armOnly then
                        local hidePos = targetHrp.Position - Vector3.new(0, 100, 0)
                        localHrp.CFrame = CFrame.new(hidePos)
                    end
                else
                    if armOnly then
                        local aimPos = targetHrp.Position
                        local rightArm = localChar:FindFirstChild("RightUpperArm") or localChar:FindFirstChild("Right Arm")
                        local leftArm = localChar:FindFirstChild("LeftUpperArm") or localChar:FindFirstChild("Left Arm")
                        if rightArm then
                            rightArm.CFrame = CFrame.new(aimPos + Vector3.new(1.5, 0, 0), aimPos)
                        end
                        if leftArm then
                            leftArm.CFrame = CFrame.new(aimPos + Vector3.new(-1.5, 0, 0), aimPos)
                        end
                        -- Scatter rest of body far away
                        if Flags["TPBodyScatter"] == true then
                            local scatterPos = aimPos + Vector3.new(0, -5000, 0)
                            local armNames = {"RightUpperArm", "RightLowerArm", "RightHand", "LeftUpperArm", "LeftLowerArm", "LeftHand", "Right Arm", "Left Arm"}
                            for _, part in ipairs(localChar:GetDescendants()) do
                                if part:IsA("BasePart") and part.Name ~= "HumanoidRootPart" then
                                    local isArm = false
                                    for _, armName in ipairs(armNames) do
                                        if part.Name == armName then isArm = true break end
                                    end
                                    if not isArm then
                                        part.CFrame = CFrame.new(scatterPos)
                                    end
                                end
                            end
                        else
                            localHrp.CFrame = targetHrp.CFrame
                        end
                    else
                        if orbit then
                            local angle = tick() * orbitSpeed * 4
                            local radius = math.max(tpAmount, 1)
                            local offset = Vector3.new(math.cos(angle) * radius, 0, math.sin(angle) * radius)
                            localHrp.CFrame = targetHrp.CFrame * CFrame.new(offset)
                        elseif tpType == "Rage" then
                            local amount = math.clamp(tpAmount, 0, 20)
                            localHrp.CFrame = targetHrp.CFrame * CFrame.new(0, 0, amount)
                        else
                            localHrp.CFrame = targetHrp.CFrame * CFrame.new(0, 0, 3)
                        end
                    end
                end
            end
        end

        if (Flags["AimbotSpectateTarget"] == true or (_bindActive and _bindActive("SpectateTargetBind"))) then
            if aimbotTarget and aimbotTarget.Character then
                local targetHum = aimbotTarget.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
                if targetHum and targetHum.Health > 0 then
                    camera.CameraSubject = targetHum
                end
            else
                local myHum = lp.Character and lp.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
                if myHum then
                    camera.CameraSubject = myHum
                end
            end
        else
            local myHum = lp.Character and lp.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
            if myHum then
                camera.CameraSubject = myHum
            end
        end
        local resolvedPart, resolvedHitPart
        if fromLockedTarget and _lockedResolvedPart and _lockedResolvedPart.Parent == target.Parent then
            local freshPart, freshHitPart = getTargetPart(target.Parent)
            if freshPart then
                -- Only reset easing if part actually changed significantly
                if _lockedResolvedHitPart and freshHitPart ~= _lockedResolvedHitPart then
                    -- Don't reset easing for every part switch - only for major switches
                    local isMajorSwitch = (_lockedResolvedHitPart == "Head" and freshHitPart ~= "Head") or
                                         (_lockedResolvedHitPart ~= "Head" and freshHitPart == "Head")
                    if isMajorSwitch and Flags["UseEasing"] then
                        _aimEaseProgress = 0
                        _aimStartCF = camera.CFrame
                        _aimStartMousePos = getMousePos()
                    end
                end
                resolvedPart = freshPart
                resolvedHitPart = freshHitPart
                _lockedResolvedPart = freshPart
                _lockedResolvedHitPart = freshHitPart
                target = freshPart
            else
                resolvedPart = _lockedResolvedPart
                resolvedHitPart = _lockedResolvedHitPart or _lockedResolvedPart.Name
                target = resolvedPart
            end
        elseif not fromLockedTarget then
            resolvedPart, resolvedHitPart = getTargetPart(target.Parent)
            if resolvedPart then
                target = resolvedPart
                if Flags["LockTarget"] or Flags["StickyAim"] then
                    _lockedResolvedPart = resolvedPart
                    _lockedResolvedHitPart = resolvedHitPart
                    lockedAimTarget = resolvedPart
                end
            end
        else
            resolvedHitPart = target.Name
            _lockedResolvedPart = target
            _lockedResolvedHitPart = resolvedHitPart
        end
        local targetPos = target.Position

        local targetHRP = target.Parent:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        if targetHRP then
            local targetKey = target.Parent:GetDebugId()
            local relOffset = targetHRP.CFrame:PointToObjectSpace(target.Position)

            if _lastPartSwitchTarget ~= targetKey then
                _lastPartSwitchTarget = targetKey
            end
            _currentPartOffset = relOffset
        end
        if Flags["UsePred"] then
            local ok, rawPredPos = pcall(getPredictedPosition, target, targetPos)
            if ok and rawPredPos then
                targetPos = rawPredPos
            end
        end
        local char = target.Parent
        local partUp = target.CFrame.UpVector
        local partHalfY = target.Size.Y * 0.5
        if resolvedHitPart == "Neck" then
            targetPos = targetPos - partUp * partHalfY
        elseif resolvedHitPart == "UpperTorso" then
            targetPos = targetPos + partUp * (partHalfY * 0.6)
        elseif resolvedHitPart == "LowerTorso" or resolvedHitPart == "Torso" then
            targetPos = targetPos + partUp * (partHalfY * 0.3)
        elseif resolvedHitPart == "HumanoidRootPart" then
            targetPos = targetPos + partUp * 0.5
        end

        local missChance = Flags["MissChance"] or 0
        targetPos = targetPos + _aimbotMissOffset

        local targetParent = target and target.Parent
        local targetPlr = targetParent and Players:GetPlayerFromCharacter(targetParent)
        _lastAimbotTargetPlayer = targetPlr
        _lastAimbotTargetPos = targetPos
        local aimPos = targetPos

        if Flags["RealisticAim"] then
            local targetChar = target.Parent
            if targetChar then
                local targetHrp = targetChar:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                if targetHrp then
                    local bobAmt = (Flags["RealisticBob"] or 3) / 10
                    local groundOff = (Flags["RealisticGround"] or 6) / 10
                    local airUpOff = (Flags["RealisticAirUp"] or 18) / 10
                    local airDownOff = (Flags["RealisticAirDown"] or 22) / 10
                    local t = tick()
                    local breathe = math.sin(t * 1.8) * bobAmt * 0.4 + math.sin(t * 3.1) * bobAmt * 0.2
                    local sway = math.cos(t * 1.2) * bobAmt * 0.15
                    local camPos = camera.CFrame.Position
                    local toTarget = (targetHrp.Position - camPos)
                    local dist = toTarget.Magnitude
                    
                    -- Check if target is in air
                    local targetHum = targetChar:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
                    local targetInAir = targetHum and targetHum.FloorMaterial == Enum.Material.Air
                    local targetVelY = targetHrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity.Y
                    
                    -- Calculate vertical offset based on target state
                    local yOffset = 0
                    if targetInAir then
                        if targetVelY > 2 then
                            -- Target jumping up - aim below to track realistically
                            yOffset = -airUpOff
                        elseif targetVelY < -2 then
                            -- Target falling - aim below
                            yOffset = -airDownOff
                        end
                    else
                        -- Grounded - apply ground offset based on camera height
                        local heightDiff = camPos.Y - targetHrp.Position.Y
                        if heightDiff > 3 and dist > 5 then
                            yOffset = -math.clamp(heightDiff / dist * groundOff, 0, groundOff)
                        end
                    end
                    
                    aimPos = aimPos + Vector3.new(sway, breathe + yOffset, 0)
                end
            end
        end

        local lockMethod = Flags["LockMethod"] or "Camera"
        local camCF = camera.CFrame
        if (aimPos - camCF.Position).Magnitude < 0.5 then return end

        _aimbotSetCamera = true
        if _unlockFade and _unlockFade < 1 then
            _unlockFade = math.min(_unlockFade + dt * 3, 1)
        end

        local getAimBlend = function(smoothValue, speedValue, dt)
            local smooth = smoothValue or 3
            if smooth <= 1.05 then return 1 end
            local speed = speedValue or 85
            dt = math.max(dt or 0.016, 0.001)
            -- Low smooth (1-25): fast and blatant, high alpha
            -- High smooth (50+): loose and smooth, low alpha
            local base = (100 / smooth) * (speed / 50)
            local alpha = 1 - math.exp(-dt * base)
            -- Boost for low smooth values to feel fast and blatant
            if smooth <= 25 then
                alpha = alpha + (1 - alpha) * 0.5
            end
            return math.clamp(alpha, 0.001, 0.999)
        end


        local easingActive = false
        if Flags["UseEasing"] and _aimEaseProgress < 1 then
            local speed = math.clamp(Flags["EasingSpeed"] or 50, 1, 100)
            local duration = math.clamp(10 / speed, 0.01, 2)
            _aimEaseProgress = math.min(_aimEaseProgress + dt / duration, 1)
            local easedT = applyEasing(_aimEaseProgress)
            if _aimStartCF then
                local targetCF = CFrame.lookAt(camCF.Position, aimPos)
                if _unlockFade and _unlockFade < 1 then
                    targetCF = camCF:Lerp(targetCF, _unlockFade)
                end
                local finalCF = _aimStartCF:Lerp(targetCF, easedT)
                if lockMethod == "Camera" then
                    camera.CFrame = finalCF
                else
                    local screenPos, onScreen = camera:WorldToViewportPoint(aimPos)
                    if onScreen then
                        local mousePos = getMousePos()
                        local dx = (screenPos.X - mousePos.X) * easedT * (_unlockFade or 1)
                        local dy = (screenPos.Y - mousePos.Y) * easedT * (_unlockFade or 1)
                        if math.abs(dx) > 0.3 or math.abs(dy) > 0.3 then
                            if mousemoverel then mousemoverel(dx, dy) end
                        end
                    end
                end
                _lastAimbotCF = camera.CFrame
                return
            end
            easingActive = true
        end

        if lockMethod == "Mouse" then
            local desiredScreenPos, onScreen = camera:WorldToViewportPoint(aimPos)
            if not onScreen then
                if Flags["StickyAim"] and lockedAimTarget then
                    local targetCF = CFrame.lookAt(camCF.Position, aimPos)
                    if _unlockFade and _unlockFade < 1 then
                        targetCF = camCF:Lerp(targetCF, _unlockFade)
                    end
                    if Flags["UseSmoothing"] then
                        local alpha = getAimBlend(Flags["SmoothX"] or 2, Flags["EasingSpeed"] or 50, dt)
                        camera.CFrame = camCF:Lerp(targetCF, alpha)
                    else
                        camera.CFrame = targetCF
                    end
                    _lastAimbotCF = camera.CFrame
                end
                return
            end
            local mousePos = getMousePos()
            local dx = (desiredScreenPos.X - mousePos.X) * (_unlockFade or 1)
            local dy = (desiredScreenPos.Y - mousePos.Y) * (_unlockFade or 1)

            if Flags["UseSmoothing"] then
                local alphaX = getAimBlend(Flags["SmoothX"] or 3, Flags["EasingSpeed"] or 85, dt)
                local alphaY = getAimBlend(Flags["SmoothY"] or 3, Flags["EasingSpeed"] or 85, dt)

                local moveX = dx * alphaX
                local moveY = dy * alphaY
                _mouseRemainderX = _mouseRemainderX + moveX
                _mouseRemainderY = _mouseRemainderY + moveY

                local intX = math.round(_mouseRemainderX)
                local intY = math.round(_mouseRemainderY)
                _mouseRemainderX = _mouseRemainderX - intX
                _mouseRemainderY = _mouseRemainderY - intY

                dx = intX
                dy = intY
            end
            if Flags["UsePullRes"] then
                local pullX = (Flags["PullResX"] or 0) / 100
                local pullY = (Flags["PullResY"] or 0) / 100
                
                -- Track user mouse movement for natural override
                local currentMousePos = getMousePos()
                if _lastUserMousePos then
                    local userDeltaX = currentMousePos.X - _lastUserMousePos.X
                    local userDeltaY = currentMousePos.Y - _lastUserMousePos.Y
                    
                    -- Blend user movement into aim
                    local userBlend = (Flags["SmoothPullUserBlend"] or 30) / 100
                    dx = dx + (userDeltaX * userBlend)
                    dy = dy + (userDeltaY * userBlend)
                end
                _lastUserMousePos = currentMousePos
                
                -- Apply pull resistance - smooth reduction
                dx = dx * (1 - pullX)
                dy = dy * (1 - pullY)
            else
                _smoothPullUserInfluenceX = 0
                _smoothPullUserInfluenceY = 0
                _lastUserMousePos = nil
            end

            if math.abs(dx) > 0.1 or math.abs(dy) > 0.1 then
                if mousemoverel then mousemoverel(dx, dy) end
            end
        else
            local targetCF = CFrame.lookAt(camCF.Position, aimPos)

            local alphaX = 1
            local alphaY = 1
            if Flags["UseSmoothing"] then
                alphaX = getAimBlend(Flags["SmoothX"] or 10, Flags["EasingSpeed"] or 50, dt)
                alphaY = getAimBlend(Flags["SmoothY"] or 10, Flags["EasingSpeed"] or 50, dt)
            end

            if Flags["UsePullRes"] then
                local pullX = (Flags["PullResX"] or 0) / 100
                local pullY = (Flags["PullResY"] or 0) / 100
                
                -- Track user mouse for Camera mode
                local currentMousePos = getMousePos()
                if _lastUserMousePos then
                    local userDeltaX = currentMousePos.X - _lastUserMousePos.X
                    local userDeltaY = currentMousePos.Y - _lastUserMousePos.Y
                    
                    local userBlend = (Flags["SmoothPullUserBlend"] or 30) / 100
                    -- Apply user blend to alpha for camera mode
                    alphaX = alphaX + (userBlend * 0.3)
                    alphaY = alphaY + (userBlend * 0.3)
                end
                _lastUserMousePos = currentMousePos
                
                -- Apply pull resistance for camera mode
                alphaX = alphaX * (1 - pullX)
                alphaY = alphaY * (1 - pullY)
            else
                _smoothPullUserInfluenceX = 0
                _smoothPullUserInfluenceY = 0
                _lastUserMousePos = nil
            end

            if alphaX >= 0.999 and alphaY >= 0.999 then
                camera.CFrame = targetCF
            else
                local currentLook = camCF.LookVector
                local targetLook = (aimPos - camCF.Position).Unit

                local function normalizeAngle(angle)
                    while angle > math.pi do angle = angle - 2 * math.pi end
                    while angle < -math.pi do angle = angle + 2 * math.pi end
                    return angle
                end

                local currentYaw = math.atan2(currentLook.X, -currentLook.Z)
                local currentPitch = math.asin(math.clamp(currentLook.Y, -1, 1))
                local targetYaw = math.atan2(targetLook.X, -targetLook.Z)
                local targetPitch = math.asin(math.clamp(targetLook.Y, -1, 1))

                local deltaYaw = normalizeAngle(targetYaw - currentYaw)
                local deltaPitch = targetPitch - currentPitch
                local newYaw = currentYaw + deltaYaw * alphaX
                local newPitch = currentPitch + deltaPitch * alphaY

                local newLook = Vector3.new(
                    math.sin(newYaw) * math.cos(newPitch),
                    math.sin(newPitch),
                    -math.cos(newYaw) * math.cos(newPitch)
                )
                camera.CFrame = CFrame.lookAt(camCF.Position, camCF.Position + newLook, Vector3.new(0, 1, 0))
            end
        end
        _lastAimbotCF = camera.CFrame
    end)))
end)

_silentLockedTarget = nil
_silentCurrentPlayer = nil
_silentRayParams = RaycastParams.new()
_silentRayParams.FilterType = Enum.RaycastFilterType.Exclude

hasSilentCheck = (function(tbl, val)
    if type(tbl) ~= "table" then return false end
    if tbl[val] == true or (tbl[val] ~= nil and tbl[val] ~= false) then return true end
    for _, v in ipairs(tbl) do
        if v == val then return true end
    end
    for k, v in pairs(tbl) do
        if k == val and v == true then return true end
    end
    return false
end)

_silentCheckChar = (function(char, checkDead, checkKnocked, checkWall, aimType, silentPriority, useFov, fovSize, ref, camPos, myChar, bestDist)
    local hum = char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
    if not hum or hum.Health <= 0 then return nil end
    if checkDead and (hum:GetState() == Enum.HumanoidStateType.Dead or isDeadCheck(char)) then return nil end
    if checkKnocked and isKnockedOrKO(char) then return nil end
    if hasSpawnProtection(char) then return nil end
    local hitPartName = Flags["SilentHitPart"] or "Head"
    local part
    if hitPartName == "Closest Part" then
        part = getClosestBodyPart(char)
    elseif hitPartName == "Torso" then
        part = char:FindFirstChild("UpperTorso") or char:FindFirstChild("Torso") or char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    elseif hitPartName == "Legs" then
        part = char:FindFirstChild("LeftUpperLeg") or char:FindFirstChild("RightUpperLeg") or char:FindFirstChild("Left Leg") or char:FindFirstChild("Right Leg")
    else
        part = char:FindFirstChild(hitPartName)
        if not part then
            if hitPartName == "UpperTorso" or hitPartName == "LowerTorso" then
                part = char:FindFirstChild("Torso") or char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
            elseif hitPartName == "Neck" then
                part = char:FindFirstChild("Head")
            end
        end
    end
    if not part then
        part = char:FindFirstChild("Head") or char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") or char:FindFirstChild("Torso") or char:FindFirstChild("UpperTorso") or char:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("BasePart")
    end
    if not part or not part.Parent then return nil end
    local partPos = getHitboxTrackedPosition(char, part)
    local screenPos, onScreen = camera:WorldToViewportPoint(partPos)
    if not onScreen then return nil end
    local screenVec = Vector2New(screenPos.X, screenPos.Y)
    local dist
    if silentPriority == "Distance" then
        local myHrp = myChar and (myChar:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") or myChar:FindFirstChild("Head") or myChar:FindFirstChild("Torso"))
        if not myHrp then return nil end
        local hrp = char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") or char:FindFirstChild("Head") or char:FindFirstChild("Torso") or part
        if not hrp then return nil end
        dist = (myHrp.Position - hrp.Position).Magnitude
    else
        dist = (screenVec - ref).Magnitude
    end
    if useFov and dist > fovSize then return nil end
    if dist < bestDist then
        if checkWall and myChar then
            _silentRayParams.FilterDescendantsInstances = {myChar, char}
            local result = workspace:Raycast(camPos, partPos - camPos, _silentRayParams)
            if result then return nil end
        end
        return part, dist
    end
    return nil
end)

local function GetTarget()

local function GetTarget()
    local player = Players.LocalPlayer
    if not player then return nil end
    local character = player.Character or player.CharacterAdded:Wait()
    if not character then return nil end

    local origin = character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    if not origin then return nil end

    local originPos = origin.Position
    local best, bestDist = nil, math.huge
    local players = Players

    local bots = Workspace:FindFirstChild("Bots")
    if bots then
        for _, v in pairs(bots:GetChildren()) do
            if v:IsA("Model") and v.Name:match("Bot_") then
                local part = v:FindFirstChild("Head") or v:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                if part and part:IsA("BasePart") then
                    local dist = (part.Position - originPos).Magnitude
                    if dist < bestDist then
                        bestDist = dist
                        best = part
                    end
                end
            end
        end
    end

    for _, v in ipairs(players:GetPlayers()) do
        if v ~= player and v.Character and v.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") then
            local part = v.Character:FindFirstChild("Head") or v.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
            if part and part:IsA("BasePart") then
                local dist = (part.Position - originPos).Magnitude
                if dist < bestDist then
                    bestDist = dist
                    best = part
                end
            end
        end
    end

    return best, bestDist
end

local _silentLastTarget = nil
local _silentLastTargetTime = 0
local function GetSilentTarget()
    local player = Players.LocalPlayer
    if not player then return nil end
    local character = player.Character
    if not character then return nil end

    local camera = Workspace.CurrentCamera
    if not camera then return nil end

    -- If sync is enabled, use cached aimbot target directly (avoid double scanning)
    local syncAimbot = Flags["SilentSyncAimbot"] == true or Flags["AimbotSyncWithTarget"] == true
    if syncAimbot and cachedAimbotTarget and cachedAimbotTarget.Parent then
        -- Just verify the target is still valid, don't do full scan
        local checkDead = hasCheck(Flags["SilentChecks"] or {}, "Dead")
        local checkKnocked = hasCheck(Flags["SilentChecks"] or {}, "Knocked")
        local char = cachedAimbotTarget.Parent
        local hum = char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
        
        if hum and hum.Health > 0 then
            if checkDead and (hum:GetState() == Enum.HumanoidStateType.Dead or isDeadCheck(char)) then
                return nil
            end
            if checkKnocked and isKnockedOrKO(char) then
                return nil
            end
            return cachedAimbotTarget
        end
        return nil
    end

    local now = tick()
    local scanInterval = 0.04
    if _silentLastTargetTime ~= 0 and now - _silentLastTargetTime < scanInterval then
        return _silentLastTarget
    end
    _silentLastTargetTime = now

    local mousePos = getMousePos()
    local mid = Vector2New(mousePos.X, mousePos.Y)
    local center = Vector2New(camera.ViewportSize.X / 2, camera.ViewportSize.Y / 2)

    local checks = Flags["SilentChecks"] or {}
    local checkDead = hasCheck(checks, "Dead")
    local checkKnocked = hasCheck(checks, "Knocked")
    local checkWall = hasCheck(checks, "Wall")
    local checkTeam = hasCheck(checks, "Team")
    local checkEnemy = hasCheck(checks, "Enemy")
    local checkNPC = hasCheck(checks, "NPC")
    local silentPriority = Flags["SilentPriority"] or "Closest Point"
    local aimType = Flags["SilentAimType"] or "Cursor"
    local useFov = Flags["SilentUseFOV"] == true
    local fovSize = tonumber(Flags["SilentFOVSize"]) or 100
    local hitChanceEnabled = Flags["SilentHitChanceEnabled"] == true
    local hitChance = math.clamp(Flags["SilentHitChance"] or 100, 1, 100)
    local lockTarget = Flags["SilentTargetLock"] == true
    local ref = (aimType == "Center" or aimType == "FOV") and center or mid

    if hitChanceEnabled and math.random(100) > hitChance then
        return nil
    end

    local bestPart, bestDist = nil, math.huge
    local myChar = character
    local camPos = camera.CFrame.Position

    local getCharacterFn = getCharacter
    local players = Players
    local localPlayer = player
    local localTeam = localPlayer.Team

    local function testCharacter(char)
        if not char then return end
        local hrp = char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") or char:FindFirstChild("Torso") or char:FindFirstChild("Head")
        if hrp and hrp.Position.Y < -80 then return end
        local plr = Players and Players:GetPlayerFromCharacter(char)
        if plr then
            local isTeammate = false
            if localTeam ~= nil and plr.Team == localTeam then
                isTeammate = true
            end
            if localPlayer.Neutral or plr.Neutral or (localTeam and (localTeam.Name == "Neutral" or localTeam.Name == "Spectators" or localTeam.Name == "Spectator")) or (plr.Team and (plr.Team.Name == "Neutral" or plr.Team.Name == "Spectators" or plr.Team.Name == "Spectator")) then
                isTeammate = false
            end
            if checkTeam and isTeammate then return end
            if checkEnemy and not isTeammate then return end
        end

        local part, dist = _silentCheckChar(char, checkDead, checkKnocked, checkWall, aimType, silentPriority, useFov, fovSize, ref, camPos, myChar, bestDist)
        if part and dist and dist < bestDist then
            bestDist = dist
            bestPart = part
        end
    end

    if lockTarget and _silentLockedTarget then
        local lockedChar = _silentLockedTarget.Parent
        if lockedChar and lockedChar:IsA("Model") then
            local part, dist = _silentCheckChar(lockedChar, checkDead, checkKnocked, checkWall, aimType, silentPriority, useFov, fovSize, ref, camPos, myChar, math.huge)
            if part and dist then
                _silentLockedTarget = part
                return part
            end
        end
        _silentLockedTarget = nil
    end

    for _, plr in ipairs(players:GetPlayers()) do
        if plr ~= localPlayer then
            local char = getCharacterFn(plr)
            if char then
                testCharacter(char)
            end
        end
    end

    if checkNPC then
        for _, npc in ipairs(getNPCs()) do
            testCharacter(npc)
        end
    end

    if lockTarget then
        _silentLockedTarget = bestPart
    else
        _silentLockedTarget = nil
    end

    _silentLastTarget = bestPart
    return bestPart
end

local function patchAimHandler()
    local Players = game:GetService("Players")
    local Player = Players.LocalPlayer
    if not Player then return end
    local Character = Player.Character or Player.CharacterAdded:Wait()
    if not Character then return end

    local PlayerScripts = Player:FindFirstChild("PlayerScripts")
    if PlayerScripts then
        local Modules = PlayerScripts:FindFirstChild("Modules")
        if Modules then
            local AimHandler = Modules:FindFirstChild("AimHandler")
            if AimHandler then
                local ok, aim = pcall(require, AimHandler)
                if ok and type(aim) == "table" and type(aim.GetAim) == "function" then
                    local originalGetAim = aim.GetAim
                    aim.GetAim = function(p15, p16)
                        if not isArsenal and Flags["SilentEnabled"] and _bindActive("SilentBind") then
                            local target = GetSilentTarget()
                            if target then
                                return target.Position, (target.Position - p15).Magnitude
                            end
                        end
                        return originalGetAim(p15, p16)
                    end
                end
            end
        end
    end
end

end

task.spawn(function()
    pcall(patchAimHandler)
end)

local getSilentAimTargetFunc = (function()
    if not _bindActive("SilentBind") or not Flags["SilentEnabled"] then return nil end
    return GetSilentTarget()
end)

getSilentAimTarget = getSilentAimTargetFunc

local _silentHooksInstalled = false
local silentTarget = nil
local silentTargetPart = nil
local silentAimTarget = nil
local _silentOldIndex = nil
local _cachedPing = 0
local _pingCacheTime = 0

local function isRemoteSignal(obj)
    return typeof(obj) == "Instance" and (obj:IsA("RemoteEvent") or obj:IsA("RemoteFunction"))
end

_getPing = function()
    local now = tick()
    if now - _pingCacheTime < 1 then return _cachedPing end
    _pingCacheTime = now
    local success, result = pcall(function()
        local ps = Stats:FindFirstChild("PerformanceStats")
        if ps then
            local pingStat = ps:FindFirstChild("Ping")
            if pingStat then
                if type(pingStat.Value) == "number" then
                    return pingStat.Value
                elseif typeof(pingStat) == "userdata" and pcall(function() return pingStat.GetValue end) then
                    local ok, v = pcall(function() return pingStat:GetValue() end)
                    if ok and type(v) == "number" then return v end
                end
            end
        end
        return nil
    end)
    if success and type(result) == "number" then _cachedPing = result end
    return _cachedPing
end

local function _installSilentHooks()
    if _silentHooksInstalled then return end
    _silentHooksInstalled = true

    local Players = game:GetService("Players")
    local lp = Players.LocalPlayer
    local mouse = lp and lp.GetMouse and lp:GetMouse() or nil

    if getrawmetatable and hookfunction and newcclosure then
        pcall(function()
            local raw_mt = getrawmetatable(game)
            if raw_mt then
                local clone = {}
                for k, v in pairs(raw_mt) do
                    clone[k] = v
                end
                local oldGetrawmetatable
                oldGetrawmetatable = hookfunction(getrawmetatable, newcclosure(function(obj)
                    if not checkcaller() then
                        if obj == game or obj == workspace or obj == lp or obj == mouse then
                            return clone
                        end
                        local mt = oldGetrawmetatable(obj)
                        if mt == raw_mt then
                            return clone
                        end
                    end
                    return oldGetrawmetatable(obj)
                end))
                local oldIndex = raw_mt.__index
                pcall(function() setreadonly(raw_mt, false) end)
                raw_mt.__index = newcclosure(_LPH_NV(function(self, key)
                    if not isArsenal and not checkcaller() and silentTarget and _bindActive("SilentBind") and Flags["SilentEnabled"] then
                        if self == mouse then
                            if key == "Hit" then
                                return CFrame.new(silentTarget)
                            elseif key == "Target" then
                                return silentTargetPart
                            end
                        end
                    end
                    return oldIndex(self, key)
                end))
                pcall(function() setreadonly(raw_mt, true) end)
            end
        end)
    end
end

task.spawn(function()
    task.wait(2)

    _installSilentHooks()

    local _silentLastUpdate = 0
    _trackConn(RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(_LPH_NV(function()
        if not _bindActive("SilentBind") or not Flags["SilentEnabled"] then
            if silentTarget ~= nil then
                silentTarget = nil
                silentTargetPart = nil
                silentAimTarget = nil
                _silentCurrentPlayer = nil
            end
            return
        end
        local now = tick()
        -- Reduce update frequency to improve FPS (0.016 = ~60fps, 0.033 = ~30fps)
        if now - _silentLastUpdate < 0.033 then return end
        _silentLastUpdate = now
        
        -- Use cached aimbot target if sync is enabled to avoid double scanning
        if Flags["SilentSyncAimbot"] and cachedAimbotTarget then
            silentTarget = cachedAimbotTarget.Position
            silentTargetPart = cachedAimbotTarget
            local targetParent = cachedAimbotTarget.Parent
            silentAimTarget = targetParent and Players:GetPlayerFromCharacter(targetParent)
            _silentCurrentPlayer = silentAimTarget
        else
            local target = getSilentAimTarget()
            if target then
                silentTarget = target.Position
                silentTargetPart = target
                local targetParent = target.Parent
                silentAimTarget = targetParent and Players:GetPlayerFromCharacter(targetParent)
                _silentCurrentPlayer = silentAimTarget
            else
                silentTarget = nil
                silentTargetPart = nil
                silentAimTarget = nil
                _silentCurrentPlayer = nil
            end
        end
    end)))
end)

EspLibrary = {
    ['Cache'] = {},
    ['Threads'] = {},
    ['Connections'] = {},
    ['Holder'] = nil
}

local espMeta = {}
espMeta.__index = (function(t, key)
    return rawget(EspLibrary, key)
end)
setmetatable(EspLibrary, espMeta)

SmallestPixel = Library.Font
TahomaBold = Library.Font
Tahoma = Library.Font
ProggyClean = Library.Font
ProggyTiny = Library.Font
UIFont = Library.Font

function EspLibrary:CreateObjects(Name, Prop)
    local New = Instance.new(Name)
    for Property, Value in Prop or {} do
        New[Property] = Value
    end
    return New
end

function EspLibrary:CreateThreads(Name, Signal, Callback)
    if not Signal or (typeof(Signal) ~= "RBXScriptSignal" and (type(Signal) ~= "table" or type(Signal.Connect) ~= "function")) then
        return nil
    end
    local Connection = Signal:Connect(Callback)
    self.Threads[Name] = Connection
    return Connection
end

parentGui = (function()
    if gethui then local ok, res = pcall(gethui); if ok and res then return res end end
    local coreGui = game:GetService("CoreGui")
    local ok = pcall(function() local f = Instance.new("Folder"); f.Parent = coreGui; f:Destroy() end)
    if ok then return coreGui end
    return Players.LocalPlayer and Players.LocalPlayer:FindFirstChildOfClass("PlayerGui")
end)()
EspLibrary.Holder = EspLibrary:CreateObjects("ScreenGui", {
    Name = "\n",
    Parent = parentGui,
    ScreenInsets = Enum.ScreenInsets.DeviceSafeInsets,
    ZIndexBehavior = Enum.ZIndexBehavior.Global,
    ResetOnSpawn = false,
    DisplayOrder = 10000,
    IgnoreGuiInset = true,
})
pcall(function()
    if not EspLibrary.Holder.Parent or not EspLibrary.Holder.Parent:IsA("Instance") then
        local pg = Players.LocalPlayer and Players.LocalPlayer:FindFirstChildOfClass("PlayerGui")
        if pg then EspLibrary.Holder.Parent = pg end
    end
end)
